Periodic PyTorch and CPU LAMMPS Al MEAM comparison

One16384-atom MEAM NPT melt at1325K50ps, then450K5ps; matched float64 CPU/GPU200ps BAOAB with identical noise,1fs integration,0.1ps observations. Short NVE fidelity gate and separate native LAMMPS timing.

research1 metadata gaps
